1,373 plugins, story-coding-agent to synapse-applications. [Back to the index](../README.md)

- **story-coding-agent**: Single-ticket implementation coordinator — fetches a Jira or Azure DevOps Boards ticket, routes to domain agents via multi-signal scoring, produces story-plan.md for approval, then sequentially invokes each domain agent with cross-domain context handoff, semantic memory retrieval, and a final cross-domain review ([gagandeepp/software-agent-teams](https://github.com/gagandeepp/software-agent-teams/tree/HEAD/story/story-coding-agent))

- **story-mapping**: Jeff Patton story mapping. Horizontal backbone of user activities (4-10), vertical sub-stories / tasks prioritized top-to-bottom, horizontal release slices with walking skeleton first then thickening releases. Reveals narrative gaps, over-detail, orphan stories, broken walking skeleton. Optional persona / platform / risk / dependency annotations. Feeds user-story-generator and planning-poker-protocol. Mermaid diagrams with optional PNG export. ([SSiertsema/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/SSiertsema/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/story-mapping))

- **story-point-estimation**: Story-point methodology — what points mean (complexity × uncertainty × effort), Fibonacci-progression rationale, team-specific reference-story calibration, velocity-based forecasting as range (mean / min / max / std), anti-pattern surfacing (hours-to-points, velocity-as-productivity, cross-team comparison, inflation). Four modes: estimate / calibrate / forecast / audit. Distinct from planning-poker-protocol (this is the criteria). Mermaid diagrams with optional PNG export. ([SSiertsema/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/SSiertsema/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/story-point-estimation))
